Color identity: RGWU | Bracket: Ruthless | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {6}{U}{U} each turn
Produces: Infinite turns; Lock
Start: Thor, God of Thunder (battlefield); Walk the Aeons (hand); Roon of the Hidden Realm (battlefield: without summoning sickness)
Steps: Cast Walk the Aeons by paying its mana cost.
  Thor's second ability triggers, dealing damage to any target.
  Resolve Walk the Aeons, causing you to take an extra turn after this one
  Blink Thor using Roon of the Hidden Realm.
  When Thor enters, it triggers, exiling Walk the Aeons from your graveyard and allowing you to cast it from exile until the end of your next turn.
  Move to your next turn.
  Cast Walk the Aeons from exile by paying its mana cost.
  Repeat from step 2.

## Combo 5315-5929-6871-6879
Cards: Toph, the First Metalbender + Haru, Hidden Talent + Ravenous Intruder + Leyline of Transformation
Color identity: RGWU | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite artifact sacrifice triggers; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers; Infinitely large creature until end of turn
Start: Toph, the First Metalbender (battlefield); Haru, Hidden Talent (battlefield); Ravenous Intruder (battlefield); Leyline of Transformation (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Leyline of Transformation has "Ally" chosen.
  You have an artifact creature in hand with the mana to cast it.
Steps: Cast any artifact creature by paying its mana cost.
  When the artifact creature enters as an Ally due to Leyline of Transformation, Haru triggers, earthbending the artifact creature, as it's a land due to Toph.
  Activate Ravenous Intruder by sacrificing the artifact creature.
  When the creature dies, Haru triggers, returning the creature from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  When the creature enters as an Ally due to Leyline of Transformation, Haru triggers, earthbending the creature, as it's a land due to Toph.
  Resolve the Ravenous Intruder ability, giving Ravenous Intruder +2/+2 until end of turn.
  Repeat from step 3.

## Combo 5344-5447-6349
Cards: Charismatic Conqueror + The Twelfth Doctor + Case of the Locked Hothouse
Color identity: RGWU | Bracket: Spicy | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {1}{W}
Produces: Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature tokens; Infinite creature ETB for target opponent; Infinite creature tokens for target opponent
Start: Charismatic Conqueror (library: on top of your library); The Twelfth Doctor (battlefield); Case of the Locked Hothouse (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You have not cast a spell from anywhere but your hand this turn.
  You have solved Case of the Locked Hothouse.
Steps: Cast Charismatic Conqueror from the top of your library by paying {1}{W}.
  Charismatic Conqueror's demonstrate ability triggers, causing you and an opponent to copy it.
  Resolve the opponent's copy of Charismatic Conqueror, and then resolve your copy of Charismatic Conqueror.
  When your Charismatic Conqueror token enters the battlefield,  the opponent's Charismatic Conqueror triggers, causing you to tap your Charismatic Conqueror token.
  Resolve the original Charismatic Conqueror spell.
  When the nontoken Charismatic Conqueror enters the battlefield, the opponent's Charismatic Conqueror triggers, causing you to not tap the nontoken Charismatic Conqueror, and causing the opponent to create a 1/1 Vampire creature token.
  When the opponent's Vampire token enters the battlefield, both Charismatic Conquerors you control trigger, causing you to create at least one 1/1 Vampire creature token.
  When your Vampire token enters the battlefield, the opponent's Charismatic Conqueror triggers, causing you to not tap the Vampire token, and causing the opponent to create a 1/1 Vampire creature token.
  Repeat from step 7.

## Combo 64-225-5531-5694-8110
Cards: Lumra, Bellow of the Woods + Famished Worldsire + Eldrazi Confluence + Warden of the Eye + Stone-Seeder Hierophant
Color identity: RGWU | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite blinking; Infinite blinking of nonland permanents; Infinite colorless mana; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite creature tokens; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers; Infinite magecraft triggers; Infinite mana lands you control can produce; Infinite self-mill; Infinite storm count; Put all land cards from your library and graveyard onto the battlefield; Reduce the toughness of creatures opponents control to 0
Start: Lumra, Bellow of the Woods (battlefield); Famished Worldsire (battlefield); Eldrazi Confluence (hand); Warden of the Eye (battlefield); Stone-Seeder Hierophant (battlefield: without summoning sickness)
Prerequisites: Lands you control can tap to produce at least {2}{C}{C}.
Steps: Activate any number of lands by tapping them, adding at least {2}{C}{C}.
  Cast Eldrazi Confluence by paying {2}{C}{C}, selecting its second mode three times, blinking Lumra, Warden of the Eye and Famished Worldsire.
  As Famished Worldsire enters, choose to sacrifice at least the lands tapped for mana in step 1, causing Famished Worldsire to then enter with three +1/+1 counters for each land sacrificed.
  When Lumra, Warden of the Eye and Famished Worldsire enter, they each trigger.
  Resolve the Lumra trigger, causing you to mill four cards and return all land cards from your graveyard to the battlefield
  When the lands enter, Stone-Seeder Hierophant triggers that many times.
  Resolve a Stone-Seeder Hierophant trigger, untapping it.
  Holding priority, activate Stone-Seeder Hierophant by tapping it, untapping a land.
  Repeat from step 6 for each remaining trigger.
  Resolve the Warden of the Eye trigger, returning Eldrazi Confluence from your graveyard to your hand.
  Resolve the Famished Worldsire trigger, causing you to look at some cards from the top of your library and put any land cards from among them onto the battlefield
  Repeat steps 6 through 9 once.
  Repeat from step 1.
Notes: If you can generate infinite {C} once you have all the lands on the battlefield, you can forego blinking Lumra and Famished Worldsire, and instead elect to blink Warden of the Eye and choose any two other modes from Eldrazi Confluence. This can allow you to blink other creatures, create infinite Eldrazi Spawn tokens and/or reduce any number of creatures' toughness to 0.

  Resolve the Warden of the Eye trigger, returning Eldrazi Confluence from your graveyard to your hand.
  Resolve the Famished Worldsire trigger, causing you to look at some cards from the top of your library and put any land cards from among them onto the battlefield
  When the land(s) enter, Amulet of Vigor triggers that many times, untapping the land(s)
  Repeat.
Notes: If you can generate infinite {C} once you have all the lands on the battlefield, you can forego blinking Lumra and Famished Worldsire, and instead elect to blink Warden of the Eye and choose any two other modes from Eldrazi Confluence. This can allow you to blink other creatures, create infinite Eldrazi Spawn tokens and/or reduce any number of creatures' toughness to 0.

  Wheel of Sun and Moon attached to you.
Steps: Cast Lightning Storm by paying {1}{R}{R}, targeting either copy of Stormchaser Drake.
  Stormchaser Drake triggers, which due to Abundance causes you to reveal cards from the top of your library until you reveal a land card and put it into your hand.

Notes: Other players may also activate Lightning Storm to change its target, making this combo risky if you do not have additional land cards in hand to activate Lightning Storm again.

Steps: Declare the creature with Breath of Fury attached and Ghalta and Mavren as attackers.
  Ghalta and Mavren triggers, creating two creature tokens.
  Deal combat damage using the creature that has Breath of Fury attached. 
  Breath of Fury triggers, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach Breath of Fury to the creature token, then causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat phase after this one.
  Repeat each combat phase.
